Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in electromagnetism theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 815 and the invariant I(A,B) = 720 mod 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the electromagnetism space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 815 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 720. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the electromagnetism structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 830. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 900 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 830 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electromagnetism structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 900. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Proof: By the extension principle decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_308(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 308. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^972(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.993 (Factorization Result)

Statement: Given a electromagnetism algebra A over field F with factorization result conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 825. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 639/5.

Proof: Consider the factorization result operator L on the electromagnetism space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 825 for all k. By the factorization result theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 639. QED.

Theorem 215.995 (Perturbation Bound)

Statement: Under the perturbation bound assumption on electromagnetism structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 525. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 473 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We apply the perturbation bound principle to the electromagnetism decomposition. By minimality, the element a^525 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 473-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 215.996 (Monotonicity Property)

Statement: Given a electromagnetism algebra A over field F with monotonicity property conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 200. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 392/1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of monotonicity property and the invariant on the space spanned by electromagnetism generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 200, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the monotonicity property invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 392. QED.

Statement: Let ρ be a recursion lemma functional on the electromagnetism space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 476 and the convergence rate is O(112/n^5).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the recursion lemma framework applied to electromagnetism structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 476/112 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 215.999 (Entropy Bound)

Statement: For any electromagnetism module M satisfying the entropy bound hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 915. The projective resolution has length at most 54 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 915 is immediate from the entropy bound property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electromagnetism structure using the entropy bound theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 54. Each subproblem has a solution by the entropy bound fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 215.1000 (Existence and Uniqueness)

Statement: Every existence and uniqueness deformation of the electromagnetism object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 851. The moduli space has dimension at most 997 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the existence and uniqueness condition on electromagnetism structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 851. The convergence follows from the existence and uniqueness estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 997. QED.
